1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any geology module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 461. The projective resolution has length at most 337 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 461 + O(1/n^337). Summing over all modes and applying the geology convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
